# Canvas pages: visible text

Plain-text dump of every page on the design canvas (https://claude.ai/artifact/8mYZLpBitBu4WNjd5FWVMn, pages "DoD Studio facelift" and "Review fixes"), as of 2026-11-05. Template and script noise is stripped but some remains. Use it to check the design without access to the canvas.
